==========================================================================================

HRS 2020 Final Release

Note to Users: This codebook is designed to match the distribution dataset. 
Codebook metadata are derived from the data collection instrument.
If you have questions concerning codebook layout or content please bring
them to the attention of the HRS staff at hrsquestions@umich.edu.

Printing recommendation: Set margins (left/right/top/bottom) to .5 inch; print
in portrait orientation using a mono-space 10-point font.

==========================================================================================

RQ016                         R INCOME FROM SELF EMPLOYMENT - MIN
         Section: Q     Level: Household       Type: Numeric    Width: 6   Decimals: 0

         (Thinking about your self-employment income in  [Last Calendar Year]):

RQ400                         HH FOOD STAMPS SINCE LAST IW
         Section: Q     Level: Household       Type: Numeric    Width: 2   Decimals: 0
         Ref: SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q400_

         Did you or other family members who may have been living (here/there) receive
         government food stamps, also known as SNAP benefits, at any time [since
         [Previous Wave Month] [Prev Wave Iw Year Of Financial R]/in the last two years]?
         
         INSTR: Include immediate family members who live with R
         
         WEB-ADMINISTERED TEXT:
         Did you or other family members who may have been living there receive
         government food stamps, also known as SNAP benefits, at any time [since
         [Previous Wave Month] [Prev Wave Iw Year Of Financial R]/in the last two years]?
         
         INSTR: Only include immediate family members who live with you.

         .................................................................................
            20          -8.  Web non-response
          1563           1.  YES
          9423           5.  NO
            31           8.  DK (Don't Know); NA (Not Ascertained)
            33           9.  RF (Refused)
           420       Blank.  INAP (Inapplicable); Partial Interview


==========================================================================================

         IF SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q400_ <> NO AND SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q400_ <> SpecialAnswer AND 
         SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q400_ <> Empty THEN 
         For SecQ.FOODSTAMPS.Q404M.i := 1 to W781_numberOfYears do
